- **trajectory-generation**: 使用本地离线几何计算单项航迹、转场与作业成本，并按求解归属和顺序生成每机航段、区域分工及航程估计。结果标记为mock，不校验地图障碍。 ([YUshire/UAV-Skills](https://github.com/YUshire/UAV-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/trajectory-generation) · ★1)
- **trajectory-generation-eval**: Evaluates the statistical fidelity and practical utility of synthetic human trajectory generation models by measuring how well generated trajectories perform on downstream mobility tasks compared to real trajectories. It probes whether synthetic data can replace real data without performance degradation across recommendation, prediction, labeling, and simulation tasks. Use when the user wants to benchmark on Foursquare Tokyo (TKY), Foursquare Istanbul (IST), Foursquare New York City (NYC), or asks about evaluating this task. Reports MAPE. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/trajectory-generation-eval) · ★3)
- **trajectory-geometry-transformer-representations**: Transformer 表征轨迹几何分析方法论 - 将计算神经科学的几何工具应用于 Transformer 可解释性研究，无需探测即可分析表征动力学 ([hiyenwong/ai_collection](https://github.com/hiyenwong/ai_collection/tree/HEAD/collection/skills/neuroscience/trajectory-geometry-transformer-representations) · ★4 · 3 copies)
- **trajectory-graph-edge-traversal**: Use when a landmark-based trajectory graph (landmark coordinates, connecting edges, and cell-to-landmark transition assignments) is already built and cells must be given a pseudo-temporal ordering along each edge of the tree. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/trajectory-graph-edge-traversal))
- **trajectory-graph-feature-evaluation**: Use when you have dynamic seed features from DELVE Step 1 and need to rank all features by how well their expression varies smoothly along the underlying cellular trajectory graph rather than by variance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/trajectory-graph-feature-evaluation))
- **trajectory-guard**: Detect agent failure spirals — repetitive loops, specification drift, and stuck trajectories — and forcibly redirect strategy. ([StepowskiEric/GrimoireStack](https://github.com/StepowskiEric/GrimoireStack/tree/HEAD/skills/execution/trajectory-guard) · ★1)
- **trajectory-guard**: Diffs actual vs expected trajectory; emits conformance report with match %, missing/extra/out-of-order steps, gate compliance. ([ndethi/faber](https://github.com/ndethi/faber/tree/HEAD/skills/trajectory-guard))
- **trajectory-hygiene**: Mid-session context stewardship - the kill-vs-continue call on a running context window, because a context has momentum and contamination never washes out. Reach for it mid-task when a session drifts or fixates on a dead idea, the topic pivots, or the window fills past the smart zone - "this session feels poisoned", "should I kill this context or keep going", "the agent keeps going back to that". Differentiator - owns only the mid-session lane; the context ending ritual is handoff's seat and doc-level economy is writing-for-agents' seat. ([Island-Dev-Crew/uncle-bob-skills](https://github.com/Island-Dev-Crew/uncle-bob-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/trajectory-hygiene) · ★4)
- **trajectory-inference**: Trajectory inference and cell fate mapping for single-cell and spatial transcriptomics: PAGA graph + diffusion pseudotime (no velocity needed), scVelo RNA velocity (stochastic / dynamical, from spliced/unspliced counts), CellRank 2 terminal states, fate probabilities and lineage drivers, then pseudotime and fates mapped on tissue with a spatial autocorrelation check. Outputs pseudotime, velocity streams, fate probability tables. Imaging panels (Xenium, MERFISH) have no unspliced counts: use PAGA/DPT or the CellRank pseudotime kernel. For a basic PAGA recipe inside a standard scRNA-seq workflo… ([jaechang-hits/SciAgent-Skills](https://github.com/jaechang-hits/SciAgent-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/genomics-bioinformatics/spatial-transcriptomics/trajectory-inference) · ★394 · 3 copies)
